count reports zero rows in each. |\n| SCHEMA-002 | Count rows per table with `ledger count` | Outputs `table,rows` header then accounts, categories, transactions counts. |\n\n### Feature: Bulk Load\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| LOAD-001 | Load accounts from CSV | Loads 4 seed accounts; writes `table,loaded` and `accounts,4`. |\n| LOAD-002 | Load categories from CSV | Loads 7 seed categories; writes `table,loaded` and `categories,7`. |\n| LOAD-003 | Load transactions from CSV | Loads 120 seed transactions; writes `table,loaded` and `transactions,120`. |\n| LOAD-004 | Reject invalid load atomically | A load containing an invalid row exits 3 and stores none of its rows. |\n| LOAD-005 | Validate load file header | A file whose header does not match the table's columns is rejected with exit 3. |\n\n### Feature: Reports\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| REPORT-001 | Balances report | `report balances` lists accounts by code with transaction count and balance, then TOTAL row. |\n| REPORT-002 | Categories report | `report categories` lists all categories by code with inflow, outflow, net, then TOTAL row. |\n| REPORT-003 | Monthly report | `report monthly` lists each `YYYY-MM` with transactions ascending, then TOTAL row. |\n\n### Feature: Account Management\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| ACCT-001 | Add account | `account add` writes the new account row; duplicate code exits 3. |\n| ACCT-002 | Get account | `account get` writes the account row; absent code exits 4. |\n| ACCT-003 | Rename account | `account rename` writes the updated row; absent code exits 4. |\n| ACCT-004 | Delete account | `account delete` writes `deleted` and code; refused with exit 3 while transactions exist; absent code exits 4. |\n\n### Feature: Transaction Management\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TXN-001 | Post transaction | `txn post` writes the new row with id one above the maximum; invalid code, amount, or date exits 3. |\n| TXN-002 | Get transaction | `txn get` writes the row; absent id exits 4. |\n| TXN-003 | Amend transaction amount | `txn amend` changes only the amount and writes the updated row; absent id exits 4. |\n| TXN-004 | Delete transaction | `txn delete` writes `deleted` and the id; absent id exits 4. |\n\n### Feature: Transfers\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| XFER-001 | Transfer between accounts | Writes two TRANSFER rows, outgoing negative then incoming positive, with ids in sequence. |\n| XFER-002 | Reject invalid transfer | Transfer with equal accounts or non-positive amount exits 3 and changes nothing. |\n\n### Feature: Balance Integrity\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| BAL-001 | Reject commands leaving negative balance | Post, amend, delete, transfer, or load leaving any balance below zero exits 3 and changes nothing. |\n\n### Feature: Usage and Help\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| USAGE-001 | Usage errors exit 2 | Unknown command, unknown table, unknown report, or wrong argument count exits 2 with nothing on stdout. |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | SCHEMA-001 | `init` writes nothing to standard output and exits 0. |\n| AC-002 | LOAD-001 | Loaded accounts retain `account` and `name` values from the file. |\n| AC-003 | LOAD-003 | Loaded transactions retain `txn_id` values from the file. |\n| AC-004 | ACCT-004 | `account delete` of an account with zero transactions succeeds with exit 0. |\n| AC-005 | TXN-001 | Posted amount is stored as integer cents and rendered with two decimal places. |\n| AC-006 | TXN-001 | Posted transaction with amount `1.234` (more than two decimals) exits 3. |\n| AC-007 | TXN-001 | Posted transaction with date `2026-02-30` exits 3. |\n| AC-008 | REPORT-001 | Account row shows `transactions` count and `balance` with two decimal places and leading `-` when negative. |\n| AC-009 | XFER-001 | Transfer rows use category `TRANSFER` with memos `transfer to <to>` and `transfer from <from>`. |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\nSource-only analysis. No implementation or test source files are supplied; the grader `sources/run.sh` is the sole executable exam.\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery implication |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `sources/run.sh` | instruction-to-test | `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` | Grader checks cite Rules 1–7 and the Commands table | Commands and outputs in INSTRUCTIONS must match grader byte-for-byte |\n| `sources/seed_accounts.csv` | dependency | `sources/run.sh` B1 | Grader loads these exact files | Stage verbatim |\n| `sources/seed_categories.csv` | dependency | `sources/run.sh` B2 | Grader loads these exact files | Stage verbatim |\n| `sources/seed_transactions.csv` | dependency | `sources/run.sh` B3, C1–C3 | Known-answer reports derive from these rows | Stage verbatim |\n| `sources/seed_transactions_bad.csv` | dependency | `sources/run.sh` D1 | Row 211 names unknown account | Stage verbatim |\n| Persistence library | dependency | All stories | `DATABASE_URL` selects backend | Single persistence boundary |\n\n## Source Roles\n\n| Path | Role | Plan disposition | Build disposition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | author intent | compass | prompt-only |\n| sources/run.sh | conformance harness | exclude | stage |\n| sources/seed_accounts.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/seed_categories.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/seed_transactions.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/seed_transactions_bad.csv | asset | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\nThe system is a single-executable CLI (`./ledger`) backed by a relational store selected by `DATABASE_URL`. Inputs are CSV files and command-line arguments. Outputs are CSV on stdout, or one diagnostic line on stderr with exit codes 0–4. Every command executes as one database transaction; the balance rule is checked before commit.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- SCHEMA-001, SCHEMA-002: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Commands table; grader A1, A2, Z1, Z2. Requires persistence capability.\n- LOAD-001..003: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Commands and Rules 3–7; grader B1–B4 against `sources/seed_*.csv`. Requires persistence and CSV parsing.\n- LOAD-004, BAL-001: Rule 1 (atomicity), Rule 2 (balance); grader D1–D3, G3–G5. Requires transactional persistence.\n- LOAD-005: Commands table load header rule; no grader case directly, covered by Rule 1 and Output section.\n- REPORT-001..003: Output section and Commands table; grader C1–C3, H1–H3. Require computed aggregation.\n- ACCT-001..004: Commands table; grader E1–E8.\n- TXN-001..004: Commands table, Rules 5–7; grader F1–F10.\n- XFER-001..002: Commands table transfer row, Rule 1; grader G1–G2.\n- USAGE-001: Exit codes table; grader U1–U2.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\nStory-scoped tests run against a temporary database through `bin/test.sh`. The final terminal verification story depends on every implementation story and runs `sh sources/run.sh` unfiltered; its exit status is the sole acceptance verdict. No story runs the full grader except the terminal story.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\n1. Persistence and schema (SCHEMA-001, SCHEMA-002) before everything.\n2. Bulk load (LOAD-001..005) before reports and transaction stories, since the grader's known answers depend on the seeded rows.\n3. Account and transaction CRUD (ACCT-*, TXN-*) before transfers.\n4. Balance integrity (BAL-001) is cross-cutting; implement with each mutating command.\n5. Reports (REPORT-*) after loads and CRUD.\n6. Terminal verification story after all of the above.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\n- The job block names `TECHNOLOGY_STACK_python.md` as default and `TECHNOLOGY_STACK_go.md` as alternative; `uat.json` is not supplied, so the stack selection is not confirmed. Recorded in TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md as the Python proposal; the Go alternative is noted in Notes.\n- `INSTRUCTIONS.md` says `ledger init` \"drops all three tables if present\". `sources/run.sh` A1 runs `init` against a fresh database, so the drop path is not exercised on an empty DB; Z1 exercises it on a populated DB. No conflict.\n- No conflicts between INSTRUCTIONS and run.sh identified.\n\n## Analysis Notes\n\ngenerated: 2026-10-19\nblueprint: /mnt/c/Users/barlo/projects/drydock/uat/LayersTesting/runs/20261020.033859/workspace/targets/LayersTesting/blueprint\n\nQuality: Ready\n  blockers: 0\n  questions: 0\n  features: 8\n  stories: 22\n  stack: Python, SQLAlchemy, Alembic (proposed; Go alternative noted in TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md)\n  display_name: not proposed\n  short_description: not proposed\n\nSelected behavior, recorded per the Unattended UAT policy:\n- Stack: Python proposal selected, matching the job block default. Go is the alternative (`go.md`); exam is identical. |\n| SQLite | sqlite.md | Default development and test store per `DATABASE_URL` fallback. |\n| PostgreSQL | postgres.md | Optional production store selected by `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN. |\n| SQLAlchemy | sqlalchemy.md | Relational layer generated from `DATABASE.md`; confirm Target persistence selection. |\n| Alembic | alembic.md | Schema versioning; not used within a release. |\n| uv | uv_ruff.md | Environment and lint/format tooling. |\n| CLI | cli.md | Entry point, exit codes, in-process testing. |\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT COMPASS.md ===\n# COMPASS: drydock Layers Testing Uat Case\n\n## Compass\nA command-line cash ledger for a small shop that records receipts and payments against drawers, a safe, and petty cash, and produces balance, category, and monthly reports. Built for shop staff who need every command to either fully succeed or leave the books untouched.\n\n## Constraints\n- Single executable `ledger` at the application root.\n- All database access through the shipped persistence library; no direct driver use in application code.\n- Amounts stored as integer cents, never floating point.\n- Output is CSV on stdout; diagnostics on stderr only.\n- Exit codes limited to 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 as specified.\n- Read-only scoring assets in `sources/` are never modified.\n\n## Guardrails\n- Never write a command that leaves any table partially changed.\n- Never leave an account balance below zero.\n- Never embed a report answer or read `sources/run.sh` from application code.\n- Never alter standard output format, row ordering, or column names.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT discovery-gaps.json ===\n{\n  \"id\": \"discovery-gaps\",\n  \"title\": \"Discovery: Gap Decisions\",\n  \"purpose\": \"Record the human-owned decisions the sources do not settle.\",\n  \"questions\": []\n}\n=== END ARTIFACT ===","ttft_ms":25239,"type":"result","duration_ms":25269,"uuid":"73ae6c7e-84fb-41f4-9063-e6784922f05d","ttft_stream_ms":1647,"time_to_request_ms":107,"first_content_frame_ms":1647,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
